<result>That's sufficient context. Writing the final review now.

### Spec Compliance

- ✅ Spec compliant. All four RPCs match the brief's signatures exactly (`konusmalarim()`, `mesajlari_getir(p_konusma_id, p_once, p_limit)`, `konusmayi_okundu_isaretle(p_konusma_id)`, `konusmayi_gizle(p_konusma_id)`), and the mandated blocking fix in `mesajlari_getir` is present and correct (`mobil/supabase/migrations/20260820134912_mesaj_okuma_rpcleri.sql:159-179`).
- ⚠️ Cannot verify from diff alone: live behavior of `konusmalarim`'s unread count, hide/unhide, and pagination against real message rows — these were deliberately not exercised with live message rows in this task (by instruction) and were left to the controller's manual check plus a later task's scenarios. Static reading confirms the logic is correct (see below), but no live assertion in this diff covers it.

### Named-risk checks

1. **Mandated blocking check** — Present at `...rpcleri.sql:166-179`, placed immediately after the membership check and before `return query`, exactly as required. It raises the identical literal `'Konusma bulunamadi'` (`:178`) as the membership-failure branch (`:163-164`) — byte-identical string, confirmed by direct comparison. Sidestep case: if the "other member" lookup (`:172-175`) returns no row (`v_diger_id is null`), the guard `v_diger_id is not null and gizli.engelli_mi(v_diger_id)` short-circuits to false and no exception is raised, falling through to `return query`, which returns all messages in that conversation with no block filter at all. For current scope this is unreachable — `mesaj_gonder` (`mesaj_gonder.sql:60-62`) always inserts both membership rows atomically when a conversation is created, `konusma_uyeleri` has `delete` revoked from `authenticated` (`konusma_uyeleri.sql:22`), and self-conversations are rejected (`mesaj_gonder.sql:20-22`) — so a birebir conversation the caller is a member of always has exactly one "other member" row. Confirmed as dead code path today, not exploitable.

2. **`konusmalarim` and blocked people** — Filter present: `not gizli.engelli_mi(d.kullanici_id)` (`...rpcleri.sql:129`), using the shared helper, not a direct `engellemeler` subquery. This is the same pattern already used by `mesajlar`'s own RLS policy (`mesajlar.sql:28`) and the check-in visibility policy, both of which explicitly document why a direct subquery causes RLS recursion (`gizli_engelli_mi_fonksiyonu.sql:26-30`). Correct and consistent.

3. **Unread count correctness** — `...rpcleri.sql:108-112`: counts only rows where `m.gonderen_id &lt;&gt; auth.uid()`, restricted to `m.konusma_id = k.id`, and `(benim.son_okuma is null or m.olusturuldu &gt; benim.son_okuma)`. Null `son_okuma` short-circuits the `or` to true, so all of the other person's messages count as unread — correct null handling, not the classic "count(*) filter with a false-on-null comparison" bug.

4. **Hidden conversations** — `benim.gizlendi_mi = false` (`:128`) filters on the caller's own membership row (`benim` is joined via `benim.kullanici_id = auth.uid()`, `:63-64` in the brief / `:115-116` in the migration), not the other member's row — correctly per-caller. `mesaj_gonder` clears `gizlendi_mi` for **both** rows on send (`mesaj_gonder.sql:71-74`), matching decision 44 ("karsi taraf yazinca konusma geri gelir"). Confirmed one person hiding does not affect the other's inbox.

5. **Page size cap** — `limit least(coalesce(p_limit, 50), 100)` (`:118` in brief numbering / `:187` in diff). Null defaults to 50 via `coalesce`. Oversized values are clamped to 100 via `least`. A negative `p_limit` (e.g. `-5`) is not clamped upward and reaches Postgres's own `LIMIT` clause, which rejects negative values with an error ("LIMIT must not be negative") — this is a hard failure, not a bypass: no caller can retrieve more than 100 rows or an unbounded set. Cap cannot be bypassed; negative input just produces an ugly raw error instead of a friendly one (minor, see below).

### Cases my test may have missed

None that change the verdict. One theoretical gap for the record: `mesajlari_getir` has no `k.tur = 'birebir'` filter (unlike `konusmalarim`), and its "other member" lookup uses `limit 1` — for a future multi-member `mekan_odasi` conversation this would check only one arbitrary other member's block status, not all of them. This is out of scope for Task 8 (`mekan_odasi` rooms don't exist yet — no INSERT path creates them), so it does not affect the current attack surface, but it is a latent trap for whoever wires up Faz 3c group rooms onto this same RPC without revisiting the check.

### Strengths

- The mandated fix is exactly what the controller specified: same helper, same error string, placed before any data is touched, with a comment explaining *why* it's needed (security-definer bypasses RLS) rather than just what it does — `...rpcleri.sql:166-171`.
- Correct instinct to reuse `gizli.engelli_mi` everywhere rather than inline `engellemeler` subqueries, avoiding the exact recursion class of bug documented from Faz 2b.
- Query shapes align with the one available index (`mesajlar_konusma_zaman` on `(konusma_id, olusturuldu desc)`): both the pagination query and the "latest message per conversation" lateral subquery filter on `konusma_id` and order by `olusturuldu desc`, which the index serves directly.
- Honest scoping in the report: it clearly separates what was proven live (auth/friendly-error paths, via `test:sema`) from what wasn't (unread/hide/paginate with real rows), rather than overclaiming coverage.
- ASCII-only constraint verified independently — no non-ASCII bytes in the new migration file.

### Issues

#### Critical (Must Fix)
None.

#### Important (Should Fix)
None.

#### Minor (Nice to Have)
- Negative `p_limit` produces a raw Postgres error ("LIMIT must not be negative") instead of a friendly message — consider `greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_limit, 50), 100))` for a fully clamped, always-valid limit (`...rpcleri.sql:187`).
- `mesajlari_getir`'s block check assumes exactly one "other member" via `limit 1` with no `tur = 'birebir'` guard — fine today, but worth a comment or a follow-up TODO before Faz 3c group rooms reuse this function, so the assumption doesn't silently break group-chat blocking later (`...rpcleri.sql:172-175`).

### Assessment

**Task quality:** Approved

**Reasoning:** The mandated security fix is implemented correctly and matches the controller's own live verification; all four named risks (blocking bypass, inbox leakage, unread-count null handling, per-caller hide scoping, page-size cap) check out against the actual SQL and the schema/RLS it interacts with. Remaining items are forward-looking robustness notes, not defects in the delivered scope.</result>

### The two disclosed items

**1. `as never` cast on `router.push`.** Acceptable and necessary given task ordering. `mobil/app.json:38` has `typedRoutes: true`, so `Href` is a closed union generated from `src/app/**`; `/sohbet/[id]` genuinely does not exist until Task 14 (`mobil/src/app/mesajlar.tsx:170` comment explains this correctly). ## Claude _2026-08-20 14:46:24 UTC_

Teknik terimi açıklamadan kullanmışım, haklısın.

**Abonelik**, uygulamanın sunucuya "bu konuşmaya yeni mesaj gelirse bana haber ver" demesi. Supabase Realtime'ın sağladığı şey bu.

Alternatifi şu olurdu: uygulama her birkaç saniyede bir "yeni mesaj var mı?" diye sorar (buna *yoklama* denir). Kötü bir yöntem — hem pil ve veri harcar, hem de mesaj en kötü ihtimalle o birkaç saniye gecikmeyle gelir. Abonelikte ise bağlantı açık kalıyor ve **sunucu** yeni satır eklenir eklenmez itiyor; mesaj anında görünüyor.

Somut olarak: B sana yazdığında, senin telefonundaki konuşma ekranı sunucuya hiçbir şey sormadan mesajı alıyor ve listeye ekliyor.

**Benim endişelendiğim şey de "kanal sızıntısı":**

Her konuşma ekranı açıldığında bir kanal açılıyor. Ekrandan çıkınca o kanalın **kapatılması** gerekiyor. Kapatılmazsa açık kalıyor ve arka planda veri almaya devam ediyor.

Kullanıcı gün içinde konuşmalar arasında gidip geliyor — mesaj kutusundan bir konuşmaya, geri, başka birine, geri. Yirmi kez girip çıkarsa yirmi açık kanal birikiyor. Hepsi bağlantı tutuyor, hepsi veri alıyor, uygulama yavaşlıyor ve pil eriyor.

**Bunu testlerin göremeyeceğini söylememin sebebi:** testler ekranı kurup çıktısına bakıyor. Hiçbir şey *başarısız olmuyor* — mesajlar doğru geliyor, ekran doğru çiziliyor. Sızıntı sessizce birikiyor ve ancak gerçek kullanımda, saatler sonra fark ediliyor. O yüzden "ekran kapanınca kanal gerçekten kapanıyor mu" sorusunu ayrıca sordurdum ve bunu **kanıtlayan bir test** istedim — yoksa kimse bakmaz.

Bu, bu turda birkaç kez karşılaştığım sınıfın aynısı: yeşil testlerin altında duran, yalnızca birinin özellikle sorması hâlinde görünen şeyler.

<result>Consistent with existing pattern. I now have enough to finalize the review.

### Spec Compliance
- ✅ Spec compliant. Screen implements decision 43/45/46 correctly: writability is derived solely from `konusmalarimiGetir()`'s `yazilabilirMi` field (no second gate query), history stays readable when read-only, unread-marking happens once a conversation is found, and the messages ordering/`FlatList inverted` usage matches the "newest first" client contract without any client-side re-sort.
- ⚠️ Cannot verify from diff: choice of `sikayetHedefId = konusmaId ?? kullaniciId` (`mobil/src/app/sohbet/[kullaniciId].tsx:374`) for `hedefId` when reporting a message. `sikayetler.hedef_id` has no FK/type discriminant validated against `mesaj_tur`, so nothing breaks mechanically, but reporting "mesaj" with a `konusma_id` (not a specific `mesaj.id`, nor even the reported user's id when no conversation exists yet) is a judgment call the implementer flagged themselves (task-14-report.md, "Endiseler" item 2). Worth a product decision before a moderation panel consumes `sikayetler` rows.
- ⚠️ Cannot verify from diff: no pagination/"load older" affordance is wired (`mesajlariGetir` is always called with default `once`/`limit`). Not required by this brief and no later task in the plan (`.superpowers/sdd/2026-08-20-faz3b-birebir-sohbet/task-*-brief.md`) appears to add it either — flagging in case it was assumed to land here.

### Named-risk checks
1. **Subscription cleanup** — verified. `useEffect` at `mobil/src/app/sohbet/[kullaniciId].tsx:337-342` returns the function `mesajlaraAbonelOl` gives back, used directly as the effect's cleanup. Test `'ekran kapaninca abonelik iptal edilir'` (`__tests__/ekranlar/sohbet/[kullaniciId].test.tsx:200-210`) calls `unmount()` and asserts `bosAbonelikIptali` (the mock's returned fn) was actually invoked — this proves the unsubscribe function runs, not merely that `mesajlaraAbonelOl` was called. Correct.
2. **Send failure preserves typed text** — verified. In `gonder()` (`[kullaniciId].tsx:348-372`), `setMetin('')` only executes inside the `try` block after a successful `await mesajGonder(...)`; on rejection the `catch` only sets `hata`, leaving `metin` untouched. Test `'gonderme reddedilirse...'` (`test.tsx:108-118`) asserts the input's `value` stays `'Merhaba'` after a rejected send. Correct.
3. **No second gate** — verified. `yazilabilirMi` (`[kullaniciId].tsx:344`) is derived only from the `konusmaSatiri` found via `konusmalarimiGetir()`, defaulting to `true` when no row exists. No import or call to `bagDurumunuGetir` anywhere in the file (confirmed by grep against the diff's import block, `[kullaniciId].tsx:270-278`). The "no conversation yet" test (`test.tsx:141-153`) drives this exact path: input stays open, first `mesajGonder` rejects, and the server's error is what surfaces. Correct, matches decision 43/45's intent to keep `mesaj_gonder` the sole authority.
4. **Read-only state** — verified, both halves. Test `'yazilabilirMi false donerse...'` (`test.tsx:130-139`) asserts the pre-existing message `'Eski mesaj'` is still rendered AND the input is gone (`queryByPlaceholderText` returns null) AND the note text is shown. The screen itself renders `FlatList` unconditionally and only swaps the input row for `KAPALI_KAPI_NOTU` (`[kullaniciId].tsx:400-419`) — history is never gated behind `yazilabilirMi`. Correct.
5. **Async testing-library usage** — verified. Every `render(...)` call (9 occurrences) and every `fireEvent.changeText`/`fireEvent.press` (8 occurrences) in the test file is preceded by `await`; `unmount()` is also awaited (`test.tsx:207`). No bare synchronous calls found.

### The two disclosed items

**(a) `SikayetHedefTuru` widened to include `'mesaj'`** (`mobil/lib/sikayet.ts:3`) — correct and minimal. The server-side union in `sikayet_gonder` (`mobil/supabase/migrations/20260820140113_realtime_ve_mesaj_sikayeti.sql:35`) already accepts `'mesaj'`; the client type was strictly a type-level gap. One line changed, nothing else in `sikayet.ts`/`sikayet.tsx` touched, and the change is exercised by an existing test file pattern (`hedefTur=mesaj` query param) consistent with `kullanici`/`check_in` usages elsewhere (`src/app/kullanici/[id].tsx:68`, `src/app/mekanlar/[id].tsx:65,99`).

**(b) Hand-editing `.expo/types/router.d.ts`** — the right disclosure, the wrong action, correctly caught and fixed by the controller before I got here. Judgment: hand-typing a generated artifact to unblock `tsc` is not an acceptable substitute for regenerating it, and this case proves why — the hand-patch was based on a stale snapshot and silently omitted the `/mesajlar` route entry, which would have surfaced as a confusing type error in the very next task rather than this one. The correct move when a generated artifact is stale in-session is one of: (1) run the generator for real (`expo start`, even briefly, as the controller ultimately did), (2) if the dev server genuinely cannot run in that environment, leave the working `as never` cast in place and say in the report "cast not removed, blocked on stale generated types" rather than fabricating generated content to make the diff look cleaner, or (3) explicitly flag the `tsc` clean-run claim as unverified pending real regeneration. Nothing in the **committed** diff depends on the hand-edit — `router.d.ts` is gitignored and never touched by this commit; the only committed change is the cast removal in `mesajlar.tsx`, which is a legitimate improvement now that the route genuinely exists, and it type-checks correctly once `router.d.ts` is regenerated normally (confirmed independently). Practice issue, not a defect in the shipped code.

### Strengths
- Clean, minimal single-purpose screen; the two `useEffect`s have a clear division of responsibility (conversation/history load vs. subscription lifecycle) and the comments at `[kullaniciId].tsx:292-296` and `332-336` explain the *why* behind non-obvious choices (no second gate, cleanup wiring) rather than restating the code.
- The stale-closure/unmount race in the history-load effect is handled properly with an `iptalEdildi` flag (`[kullaniciId].tsx:305,310,317,322,327-329`) — a fetch resolving after unmount won't call `setState` on an unmounted component.
- Redundant guard on `gonder()` (`[kullaniciId].tsx:348-352`, explicitly commented) defends against a fireEvent-style direct trigger bypassing the `disabled` prop, and the whitespace-only-message test exercises exactly that path.
- Test coverage maps 1:1 to all 7 brief-mandated scenarios plus two extra (subscribe-with-right-id-and-append, and the "no conversation, no `konusmayiOkunduIsaretle` call" negative case) — genuine behavior assertions (DOM text/values, mock call args, unsubscribe invocation), not choreography checks like "was rendered without crashing."
- Full ASCII scan of the diff (code, comments, test names) found zero Turkish diacritics or em dashes.

### Issues
#### Critical (Must Fix)
None.

#### Important (Should Fix)
None.

#### Minor (Nice to Have)
- When sending into an **already-established** conversation, the sent message is not optimistically appended — it only appears once the realtime subscription echoes the INSERT back (`gonder()` at `[kullaniciId].tsx:353-366` only refetches/sets `mesajlar` on the *first* send that creates a conversation; for subsequent sends nothing updates `mesajlar` directly). This isn't tested (the send-success test only checks `mesajGonder` was called and the input cleared, not that the message appears) and could read as a lag/glitch if realtime is slow. Not a spec violation, but worth a follow-up ticket for optimistic append.
- The `hedefId` choice for message reports (`konusmaId ?? kullaniciId`, `[kullaniciId].tsx:374`) is inconsistent in kind with the other two `hedefTur` values (`kullanici` -&gt; a user id, `check_in` -&gt; a specific check-in row id) — for `mesaj` it's a conversation id, not a specific message id, and falls back to a user id when no conversation exists. Self-flagged by the implementer; worth a decision before it's relied on.

### Assessment

**Task quality:** Approved

**Reasoning:** All five named risks are genuinely verified with real assertions, not mock choreography; the client contract and spec decisions 43/45/46 are followed precisely (single source of truth for the write gate, history preserved in read-only state, cleanup actually proven on unmount). Both disclosed items were handled correctly — the `sikayet.ts` widening is minimal and server-backed, and the generated-file hand-edit, while not best practice, was transparently disclosed, didn't leak into the commit, and was independently corrected before this diff's baseline was trusted. Remaining findings are minor/product-judgment items, not defects.</result>

Cause: `index.tsx`'s badge state is a plain number, so React bails out on same-value `setState` and only renders once; `mesajlar.tsx` always assigns a new array reference from `konusmalarimiGetir()`, so it re-renders and re-registers the focus callback, duplicating entries in a plain array. Fixed by switching that test file's registry to a `Set`.

<result>### Spec Compliance
- ✅ Spec compliant

### Per-brief file check
- Task 15 `mobil/src/app/index.tsx`: touched — `mobil/src/app/index.tsx:5,10,22-29,44-49` (import, state, effect fetch+sum, badge JSX). Matches brief.
- Task 15 `mobil/__tests__/ekranlar/index.test.tsx`: touched — 4 new tests added (`index.test.tsx:78-118`). Matches brief.
- Task 16 `mobil/src/app/kullanici/[id].tsx`: touched — `kullanici/[id].tsx:206-216`, gate condition copied verbatim from brief. Matches.
- Task 16 `mobil/__tests__/ekranlar/kullanici/[id].test.tsx`: touched — 4 new tests (`kullanici/[id].test.tsx:497-539`). Matches.
- Carried fix `mobil/src/app/mesajlar.tsx`: touched — `useEffect` → `useFocusEffect(useCallback(...))` (`mesajlar.tsx:1-3,17-25`). Landed in the commit named "mobil: ana ekranda mesajlar girisi" (only two commits exist total, and the other, 383b158, is scoped to `kullanici/[id]` per its subject; by elimination `mesajlar.tsx` is in `fcfb0fe`, consistent with report). Stated reason (bundling with Task 15's badge-staleness fix since both are messaging-badge refresh concerns) is a reasonable rationale, though the commit *subject line* itself doesn't mention the carried fix — traceability nit, not a defect.
- Carried fix test `mobil/__tests__/ekranlar/mesajlar.test.tsx`: touched, new focus-reload test at `mesajlar.test.tsx:260-276`, correctly under `__tests__/ekranlar/`.

All 6 files listed across both briefs are touched; STAT block confirms exactly 6 files changed — no extra files, nothing missing.

### Named-risk checks
1. **Badge zero-state** — verified. `index.tsx:51-54` gates both new badge (`okunmamisMesajSayisi &gt; 0`) and reuses existing `Baglar` pattern. Zero-case test at `index.test.tsx:84-95` uses `queryByText(/^\d+$/)` (would catch a literal "0" render, not just skip checking it) — this is a real zero-case assertion, not merely "positive case tested."
2. **Unread total (sum, not count)** — verified. `index.tsx:24-27`: `konusmalar.reduce((toplam, k) =&gt; toplam + k.okunmamis, 0)`. Test at `index.test.tsx:97-108` uses rows with `okunmamis: 2` and `okunmamis: 5`, asserting `'7'` — this fixture is discriminating (count would be 2, first-row would be 2, only sum gives 7), so it would catch a count/sum confusion. Satisfies the risk's own bar.
3. **Failure isolation** — verified. `index.tsx:28-29` `.catch(() =&gt; setOkunmamisMesajSayisi(0))`, matching the existing `Baglar` catch pattern. Rejection-path test at `index.test.tsx:110-118` mocks `konusmalarimiGetir` rejected and asserts `'Mesajlar'` text still renders with no numeric badge.
4. **Profile button gate** — verified against spec. Spec ("43. Yazma kapisi: karsilikli takip VEYA kabul edilmis sohbet istegi", `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-20-faz3b-birebir-sohbet-design.md:67-79`) matches the implemented condition (`takip==='kabul' || sohbet==='kabul' || gelenSohbet==='kabul'`) exactly — no narrower or broader gate than the server's `bag.yazabilir_mi`. `gelenTakip` (pending incoming follow) is correctly excluded, since per spec mutuality collapses into `takip` once accepted. Tests cover all three "shown" branches plus the "none" branch.
5. **Carried focus fix** — verified as a genuine reload proof, not registration-only. `mesajlar.test.tsx:260-276` asserts call count 1 → mutates the mock's resolved value → fires registered callbacks → asserts call count 2, and the second call's resolved data differs from the first, so the test would fail if focus only re-registered without re-fetching.
6. **Async testing-library usage** — verified. Every `render(...)` and `fireEvent.press(...)` in the diff is `await`ed (`index.test.tsx:79-81`, `kullanici/[id].test.tsx:151-152`, `mesajlar.test.tsx:263,271-273`). No bare calls found.

### The double-registration diagnosis
Correct, and the fix is honest rather than a mask. Mechanism check: in `index.tsx`, both badge counts are numeric state; when a fetch resolves to a value equal to the current one (`0 === 0`), React's same-value bailout skips the re-render, so the mock's `useFocusEffect` body (which pushes into the registry on every render pass) runs once — no duplication surfaces. In `mesajlar.tsx`, `setKonusmalar(await konusmalarimiGetir())` always assigns a new array reference, so a real re-render happens after every fetch resolves regardless of content, and the mock's naive `push(effect)`-on-every-render re-adds the same (correctly `useCallback`-stabilized) function reference to the array a second time — producing the observed 3-vs-2 call count when the array was `forEach`'d.

Important nuance the report's phrasing slightly blurs: this is an artifact of the test double, not of the real `expo-router`/navigation `useFocusEffect`, which internally re-subscribes only when the effect/navigation object reference changes (via its own `useEffect([navigation, effect])`), not on every unrelated re-render — so in production this screen does not actually double-fire on focus. The report's section header ("a real bug found") slightly overstates this as an app-level finding; it is accurately a test-scaffolding gap, which the body of the note does correctly describe. Switching the registry to a `Set` is the right fix: it dedupes only identical (stable, correctly-memoized) function references injected by the mock's per-render push, while still growing (and thus still catching) if a future regression removed the `useCallback` and produced genuinely distinct effect references each render. No real defect is masked.

### Strengths
- Zero-state and sum-vs-count tests are specifically constructed to be discriminating (not degenerate fixtures), directly closing the two likeliest failure modes named in the risk list.
- Failure-isolation pattern for the new badge is copy-consistent with the existing `Baglar` badge, reducing surface for divergence.
- The profile-button gate is copied verbatim from the brief/spec with no added conditions, correctly deferring authority to the server per spec's stated design rationale (#43).
- The carried-forward fix's root-cause investigation (documented in the report) is genuinely diagnosed correctly, and the produced comment in `mesajlar.tsx:14-17` explains the fix for future readers.

### Issues
#### Critical (Must Fix)
None.

#### Important (Should Fix)
None.

#### Minor (Nice to Have)
- `index.test.tsx`'s focus-mock registry (`index.test.tsx:23,35`) remains a plain array while `mesajlar.test.tsx`'s was switched to a `Set`. The array version carries the identical latent duplicate-push behavior; it simply isn't exercised today because every state that mock's tests drive to a `forEach` trigger stays at a same-value bailout (0). If a future test in that file adds a `forEach`-triggered refocus assertion after a real (non-bailout) state change, it would hit the same 3-vs-2 symptom. Worth converting both mocks to `Set` for consistency, not blocking.
- Report's "TDD note" heading calls the finding "a real bug," which overstates it — the note's own body correctly attributes it to the mock, not to `mesajlar.tsx`'s production behavior. Framing nit only.

### Assessment

**Batch quality:** Approved

**Reasoning:** Both briefs are implemented file-for-file with correct arithmetic, correct zero-state handling, correct failure isolation, and a gate condition that matches the spec's authoritative door exactly; the carried-forward focus fix is proven by a genuine reload assertion, and the reported test-registry fix is a sound, non-masking correction of a mock artifact.</result>

**Context window dolduğunda** oturum bitmiyor. Sistem, konuşmanın eski kısmını özetliyor ve ben özet + son bölümle devam ediyorum. Çalışmaya kaldığım yerden devam edebiliyorum.

Ama bir bedeli var: **ayrıntı kayboluyor, özet kalıyor.** Bugün yaptığımız işte bu somut olarak şu demek — "Görev 8'in incelemesinde şu bulundu, şu karara bağlandı" gibi yüzlerce ayrıntının çoğu özetin içinde erir. Neyi neden yaptığımı hatırlarım, ama hangi commit'in hangi bulguyu kapattığını hatırlamayabilirim.

Kullandığım becerinin bu konuda net bir uyarısı var: *"Konuşma hafızası sıkıştırmadan sağ çıkmaz. Gerçek oturumlarda, yerini kaybeden kontrolörler tamamlanmış görev dizilerini baştan yaptırdı."* Yani en pahalı hata, bitmiş işi bitmemiş sanmak.

**Bugün her görevden sonra deftere yazmamın sebebi tam olarak buydu.** Sıkıştırma olsaydı bile `progress.md` ve commit geçmişi yerinde kalırdı; ben de onlara bakıp devam ederdim, kendi hatırladığıma değil.

**Token kotan dolduğunda** ise iş değişiyor — o hesap düzeyinde bir sınır ve oturumu gerçekten bitiriyor. Onu ben göremiyorum; senin "kota dolmak üzere" demen benim tek bilgi kaynağımdı. O yüzden bu tür bir uyarıyı bana söylemen değerli: söylediğin anda devir yazmaya geçebiliyorum.

Bugünkü kesinti de bunu iyi gösterdi. Devir notunu yazdım, sonra Görev 17 bitti ve notu **düzeltmem** gerekti — çünkü içinde "paket kırık ve bu beklenen" yazıyordu, oysa artık yeşildi. Notu düzeltmeseydim yeni oturum çalışan bir paketi onarmaya girişirdi.

Pratik sonucu şu: bir sonraki sefer kota yaklaşırken yine haber ver, ama endişelenmene gerek yok — kaydın her görevde tutuluyor, tek bir mesajlık uyarı bile devir için yeterli.
